[u0062 | 00:05:12.980-00:05:14.379 | DM] Here's how you got here.
[u0063 | 00:05:16.210-00:05:25.189 | DM] Last spring, the embittered adventurer Hulda received a 400-year-old deed of inheritance. Perfect. Written in oddly contemporary language.
[u0064 | 00:05:25.300-00:05:29.109 | DM] To a manor house at the wild northern edges of the Duchy of Tellim.
[u0065 | 00:05:29.500-00:05:30.829 | DM] There was one condition.
[u0066 | 00:05:30.960-00:05:37.449 | DM] Go through the door in the cellar of the house, and convince the Fae lord on the other side to break a promise once made.
[u0067 | 00:05:38.370-00:05:48.809 | DM] Lord Endless Ending had been tricked into his compact by the wizard Thalestria, head of the Drankorian order Arithrimos Lamperum in the waning days of the Empire.
[u0068 | 00:05:50.300-00:05:53.389 | DM] Each time a clock she gave him struck zero.
[u0069 | 00:05:53.620-00:05:58.540 | DM] Lord Ending would send a mortal guest at his party through the doors of her house.
[u0070 | 00:05:58.910-00:06:08.679 | DM] That compact has held to the present, 700 years later, though for the Feylord, it was merely struck earlier, in the same endless celebration he still hosts.
[u0071 | 00:06:09.980-00:06:23.770 | DM] Hulda hires the party, who travel to the Feylord's Revel, where Tarek steals a yet-to-be-disclosed possession of that lord, and the party meets guests from across centuries, including a curious Rakshasa, Tigerman.
[u0072 | 00:06:24.840-00:06:27.879 | DM] The Fae is unable to break his promise.
[u0073 | 00:06:28.080-00:06:32.930 | DM] But asks them to bring him the key to the prison door at the center of the house.
[u0074 | 00:06:33.680-00:06:39.330 | DM] Presumably because if he breaks it and the prisoner goes free, his promise becomes irrelevant.
[u0075 | 00:06:40.200-00:06:49.370 | DM] Joined by Menestheus, a member of Thalestria's party who she left behind when she made the bargain, the group enters the house of 27 rooms.
[u0076 | 00:06:50.840-00:07:01.269 | DM] Surrounding a central prison chamber, the Order built the house as a magical and mechanical device to untether the central prison room from the world around it.
[u0077 | 00:07:01.360-00:07:11.910 | DM] And to keep the physical and the temporal location of that central room constantly changing. If it stopped moving somehow, the prisoner would quickly escape.
[u0078 | 00:07:12.930-00:07:20.379 | DM] Each movement through a doorway between rooms in the house shifts the physical or temporal coordinates of the prison.
[u0079 | 00:07:20.530-00:07:29.559 | DM] And in that way, the randomness of mortal choices kept the prisoner from knowing where or when they were or what to expect was coming.
[u0080 | 00:07:30.050-00:07:37.179 | DM] And the steady supply of mortals, by Lord Endless Ending, has kept it in constant motion to this day.
[u0081 | 00:07:38.670-00:07:45.929 | DM] The party makes their way through the house, fighting off fungal-transformed attackers, one of whom infects Menestheus badly.
[u0082 | 00:07:46.270-00:07:50.230 | DM] And realizes that Hulda's servant who hired them.
[u0083 | 00:07:50.230-00:07:53.689 | DM] The servants they saw at the house, too, were these same mushroom men.
[u0084 | 00:07:54.590-00:08:01.249 | DM] They meet Kallipseis, an ancient dwarven wizard, putting the final touches on a fungal golem body.
[u0085 | 00:08:01.250-00:08:07.529 | DM] Into which she intends to transfer her consciousness and restart the Arithrimos Lamperum.
[u0086 | 00:08:08.580-00:08:12.550 | DM] They collect a clock and bring it with them, which always gives the.
[u0087 | 00:08:12.550-00:08:16.659 | DM] Ever-shifting year inside of the central room.
[u0088 | 00:08:17.420-00:08:20.210 | DM] And finally, and Eric, this is what you missed last session.
[u0089 | 00:08:20.300-00:08:28.110 | DM] You come to the library and talk with a clockwork magical research assistant, who, unnamed, they give the name Aurae to.
[u0090 | 00:08:29.250-00:08:44.990 | DM] Aurae helps them to understand… sorry, I should have written it in the second person. Aurae helps you to understand that the house is powered by the energy of trapped souls prevented from moving on to their afterlife, like water trapped behind a dam.
[u0091 | 00:08:45.950-00:08:51.539 | DM] They read from the order's Arithrimos Lamperum's Allegories for the Initiated.
[u0092 | 00:08:51.910-00:08:53.780 | DM] Odd stories about…
[u0093 | 00:08:53.780-00:08:55.950 | DM] Hedgehogs made to kill.
[u0094 | 00:08:55.950-00:08:58.629 | DM] And monkey trap prisons?
[u0095 | 00:08:58.630-00:09:05.700 | DM] Where it's just large enough for the monkey to put its hand in, but when it closes its fist around what's inside and tries to take it out, it can't?

[u0368 | 00:35:29.830-00:35:33.319 | DM] About how words make before and after.
[u0369 | 00:35:35.470-00:35:39.620 | DM] How words make stories about befores and afters.

[u0371 | 00:35:44.790-00:35:54.089 | DM] And he is coming back when it has ended. And I'm next to my well for a very long time. I'm liking how still the world is.

[u0383 | 00:36:46.800-00:36:48.189 | DM] down the road?
[u0384 | 00:36:48.860-00:36:52.700 | DM] And it's walking up and up to where I am sitting.
[u0385 | 00:36:53.890-00:36:57.910 | DM] And it is speaking in words to me, and I am speaking back.

[u0395 | 00:37:54.900-00:37:56.589 | DM] What is the story of your dream?
[u0396 | 00:38:04.060-00:38:07.869 | Tarek] Once… When I was younger than I am now.
[u0397 | 00:38:10.480-00:38:11.869 | DM] I was exploring.
[u0398 | 00:38:12.460-00:38:16.140 | DM] A place I thought I knew well, near where I grew up.

[u0400 | 00:38:23.770-00:38:24.610 | Tarek] I was hurt.
[u0401 | 00:38:26.830-00:38:29.300 | DM] But in the beam of light from above.
[u0402 | 00:38:30.070-00:38:33.249 | DM] I saw some very old things.
[u0403 | 00:38:33.430-00:38:34.550 | DM] around me.
[u0404 | 00:38:35.540-00:38:40.370 | DM] And they were from… Another people.

[u0861 | 01:19:21.600-01:19:23.180 | Kaito Min] And that made me very happy.
[u0862 | 01:19:23.830-01:19:25.310 | Kaito Min] It made me very, very happy.
[u0863 | 01:19:27.140-01:19:28.310 | Kaito Min] And I'm old.

[u0865 | 01:19:36.150-01:19:43.730 | Kaito Min] I've also kept so many dreams and so many souls, and I thought, That… that made me happy.

[u0867 | 01:19:48.900-01:19:53.859 | Kaito Min] That every new soul that I kept, every new dream that I collected.
[u0868 | 01:19:54.810-01:20:00.600 | Kaito Min] Would somehow make me more full, and make me more… at peace.

[u0877 | 01:20:31.730-01:20:33.230 | Kaito Min] Here, there's nothing.
[u0878 | 01:20:35.140-01:20:37.990 | Kaito Min] There are no dreamers and no souls, for me.
[u0879 | 01:20:39.660-01:20:42.430 | Kaito Min] But something is different here for me.
[u0880 | 01:20:43.300-01:20:46.810 | Kaito Min] Something that's different here than I've felt out there.
[u0881 | 01:20:47.620-01:20:53.360 | Kaito Min] And… Even though there's no more Dreamers and no more souls.
[u0882 | 01:20:54.070-01:20:58.170 | Kaito Min] With all of that gone, now I can see who I am.
[u0883 | 01:20:59.570-01:21:03.110 | Kaito Min] And… I thought I knew what happiness was.
[u0884 | 01:21:03.570-01:21:11.920 | Kaito Min] But now, without any dreamers, without any souls, with just this nothingness, I'm not scared.
[u0885 | 01:21:12.680-01:21:15.109 | Kaito Min] I'm not… Empty, either.
[u0886 | 01:21:16.270-01:21:20.360 | Kaito Min] I think I'm… Happy for the first time.

[u0888 | 01:21:22.680-01:21:23.640 | Kaito Min] Here.
[u0889 | 01:21:24.620-01:21:26.540 | Kaito Min] in the nothingness.
[u0890 | 01:21:26.800-01:21:30.320 | Kaito Min] In the darkness. With no souls and no dreams.

[u0940 | 01:24:38.810-01:24:44.480 | Tarek] Alright, so… You said to state the intention first?

[u0942 | 01:24:48.460-01:24:58.460 | Tarek] Oh. It's about, how?
[u0943 | 01:24:58.640-01:25:02.230 | Tarek] Giving is receiving.
[u0944 | 01:25:03.650-01:25:06.930 | Tarek] And… letting go.
[u0945 | 01:25:08.530-01:25:10.940 | Tarek] is… lightness.
[u0946 | 01:25:17.980-01:25:20.929 | Tarek] Once, when I was a kid, I,
[u0947 | 01:25:21.260-01:25:30.329 | Tarek] I just acted so fast out of instinct. I reached my hand to grab the frying pan that my mom had dropped.
[u0948 | 01:25:30.600-01:25:33.639 | Tarek] She wasn't really my mom, but, you know, the family I was living with.
[u0949 | 01:25:34.100-01:25:35.630 | Tarek] Into the fireplace.
[u0950 | 01:25:36.440-01:25:41.890 | Tarek] And… you know, I've always been kind of quick, so I managed to grab it right by the handle.
[u0951 | 01:25:42.080-01:25:43.570 | Tarek] But,
[u0952 | 01:25:43.670-01:25:52.510 | Tarek] Everybody was kind of afraid and surprised when I held it up, smiling, and gave it back, because… it was burning hot.
[u0953 | 01:25:54.030-01:26:01.669 | Tarek] But I didn't feel it. Nobody understood why. They were a little afraid of me after that, and I left home
[u0954 | 01:26:02.270-01:26:04.870 | Tarek] Just for a day or two, like I would sometimes.
[u0955 | 01:26:05.080-01:26:14.909 | Tarek] And… wandering around, I've… I realized it was the… The summertime that…
[u0956 | 01:26:15.440-01:26:22.459 | Tarek] My body didn't react to the sun… the way it normally did, and I tried.
[u0957 | 01:26:22.580-01:26:23.350 | Tarek] You know.
[u0958 | 01:26:24.540-01:26:27.119 | Tarek] Juggling hot coals, I found I could.
[u0959 | 01:26:30.070-01:26:36.690 | Tarek] But one day… I took off that ring. It's the one I found when I fell through the floor.
[u0960 | 01:26:38.110-01:26:39.519 | Tarek] And it all stopped.
[u0961 | 01:26:40.120-01:26:44.570 | Tarek] I thought maybe… Maybe I should look into this.
[u0962 | 01:26:46.650-01:26:48.270 | Tarek] Before I had a chance to…
[u0963 | 01:26:50.690-01:26:53.010 | Tarek] Wonder if maybe that could be my way out.
[u0964 | 01:26:53.140-01:26:54.500 | Tarek] Maybe I could sell it.
[u0965 | 01:26:55.500-01:26:57.630 | Tarek] Maybe somebody would be amazed.
[u0966 | 01:26:57.890-01:26:59.610 | Tarek] want to learn from me where I found it.
[u0967 | 01:27:03.500-01:27:05.459 | Tarek] It was the Midsummer Festival.
[u0968 | 01:27:06.160-01:27:07.890 | Tarek] Right around then, too, and
[u0969 | 01:27:08.930-01:27:10.890 | Tarek] You know, they love the fireworks.
[u0970 | 01:27:11.080-01:27:16.139 | Tarek] In that town where I grew up, and… Sometimes there's accidents.
[u0971 | 01:27:16.300-01:27:20.420 | Tarek] And, normally.
[u0972 | 01:27:20.530-01:27:22.660 | Tarek] the kids I ran with.
[u0973 | 01:27:23.280-01:27:26.820 | Tarek] We just laugh, and… Maybe.
[u0974 | 01:27:27.600-01:27:33.100 | Tarek] Sort through the wreckage, but this night… I saw a man weeping.
[u0975 | 01:27:35.350-01:27:37.469 | Tarek] Before a burning structure.
[u0976 | 01:27:38.910-01:27:45.220 | Tarek] And he was calling out for help because… This kid was in there.
[u0977 | 01:27:47.930-01:27:51.580 | Tarek] And… Something let go inside me, and I,
[u0978 | 01:27:52.840-01:27:55.199 | Tarek] I went to him, and I gave him the ring.
[u0979 | 01:27:56.750-01:27:58.650 | Tarek] I said, look.
[u0980 | 01:27:58.890-01:28:01.940 | Tarek] Look, this will protect you. You can go.
[u0981 | 01:28:04.110-01:28:05.540 | Tarek] You can rescue her.
[u0982 | 01:28:06.810-01:28:08.010 | Tarek] And he went in.
[u0983 | 01:28:09.010-01:28:10.600 | Tarek] And he was not burned.
[u0984 | 01:28:17.280-01:28:19.560 | Tarek] And I felt something that also…
[u0985 | 01:28:26.710-01:28:28.480 | Tarek] Became calm within me.
[u0986 | 01:28:31.470-01:28:36.819 | Tarek] Because I thought maybe… That thing I found, I should find more of those things in that…
[u0987 | 01:28:40.300-01:28:42.310 | Tarek] That was all I would need in life, but…
[u0988 | 01:28:42.800-01:28:44.890 | Tarek] That moment, I realized there was more.
[u0989 | 01:28:45.930-01:28:48.190 | Tarek] I needed both to take and to give.
[u0990 | 01:28:57.310-01:29:05.490 | Tarek] And… I never saw that man again. I don't know if his child… Survived.
[u0991 | 01:29:08.050-01:29:10.300 | Tarek] But I do know that I have a feeling.
[u0992 | 01:29:10.740-01:29:13.280 | Tarek] Of having made a change in the world for the better.
[u0993 | 01:29:14.750-01:29:17.219 | Tarek] But I could only do it by giving something.
[u0994 | 01:29:18.390-01:29:19.870 | Tarek] Instead of holding it.

[u0998 | 01:29:45.370-01:29:58.969 | DM] And the give and take of… imagining possibilities and growing into shapes, where… when the teacher was teaching it how to grow itself into…
[u0999 | 01:29:59.070-01:30:06.839 | DM] Buildings, and to stretch its roots far and wide, that they… that there was an interchange there.

[u1020 | 01:31:14.190-01:31:15.730 | Tarek] Yes, I will do so.

[u1036 | 01:32:05.330-01:32:07.959 | Ayveen] And now she beats a little bit more…
[u1037 | 01:32:08.250-01:32:12.740 | Ayveen] firmly on her hand drum. She wants to…

[u1040 | 01:32:28.490-01:32:34.459 | Ayveen] to… memories… and experiences…

[u1043 | 01:32:38.820-01:32:42.100 | Ayveen] Shine that light back on how…

[u1045 | 01:32:48.950-01:32:49.920 | Ayveen] So…
[u1046 | 01:32:49.930-01:32:51.110 | DM] Ayveen…
[u1047 | 01:32:55.690-01:32:59.460 | Ayveen] Does…
[u1048 | 01:33:03.720-01:33:19.580 | Ayveen] I sing a song… All the people… Who have told me… They've seen… A meaningful light shining.
[u1049 | 01:33:21.220-01:33:24.560 | Ayveen] I've seen…
[u1050 | 01:33:24.840-01:33:26.310 | DM] A fey lord.
[u1051 | 01:33:27.490-01:33:30.159 | Ayveen] Thinking in endless twilight.
[u1052 | 01:33:31.170-01:33:32.340 | DM] of…
[u1053 | 01:33:32.680-01:33:34.810 | Ayveen] Stars in the sky.
[u1054 | 01:33:36.770-01:33:40.270 | Ayveen] I've seen… The moon.
[u1055 | 01:33:41.390-01:33:43.210 | Ayveen] Shining so bright.
[u1056 | 01:33:45.310-01:33:49.820 | Ayveen] And… Making people think.
[u1057 | 01:33:51.190-01:33:53.290 | Ayveen] Of the day that is to come.
[u1058 | 01:33:53.690-01:34:00.929 | Ayveen] On the day that was passed, And… a night for dreaming.
[u1059 | 01:34:02.780-01:34:05.940 | Ayveen] And the lights twinkle in the sky.
[u1060 | 01:34:06.190-01:34:07.470 | Ayveen] the stars.
[u1061 | 01:34:07.870-01:34:12.250 | Ayveen] They have a rhythm of their own in the pulse of the twinkle.
[u1062 | 01:34:13.150-01:34:22.610 | Ayveen] And when it's daytime and you can't see them, You still know… That particular bright light.
[u1063 | 01:34:22.810-01:34:24.390 | Ayveen] of the stars.
[u1064 | 01:34:25.100-01:34:29.140 | Ayveen] And when it's… Nighttime, but there's no moon.
[u1065 | 01:34:29.990-01:34:34.069 | Ayveen] You still know… That particular radiance.
[u1066 | 01:34:34.870-01:34:37.069 | Ayveen] Of being bathed in that.
[u1067 | 01:34:37.590-01:34:38.980 | Ayveen] Soft light.
[u1068 | 01:34:41.510-01:34:50.140 | Ayveen] And… All of these lights… come to different people, and they mean so much, and they're not gone.
[u1069 | 01:34:50.650-01:34:52.120 | Ayveen] When they're not there.
[u1070 | 01:34:53.950-01:34:58.769 | Ayveen] And… In the Feywild at Twilight.
[u1071 | 01:34:59.600-01:35:02.710 | Ayveen] Or… In the dark of a well.
[u1072 | 01:35:04.080-01:35:08.559 | Ayveen] There's always the thought… Of the bright shining.
[u1073 | 01:35:08.670-01:35:17.930 | Ayveen] of… The lights that we've seen, The souls that we've met, And the rhythm…
[u1074 | 01:35:18.570-01:35:21.260 | Ayveen] Of their movement in our memories.
[u1075 | 01:35:23.990-01:35:26.980 | Ayveen] And our hope that we will see them again.
[u1076 | 01:35:28.630-01:35:30.700 | Ayveen] And our knowing that they are…
[u1077 | 01:35:31.840-01:35:35.069 | Ayveen] And they have been. And they will be.
[u1078 | 01:35:39.910-01:35:41.729 | Ayveen] And we see the stars.
[u1079 | 01:35:42.030-01:35:43.539 | Ayveen] In the daytime.
[u1080 | 01:35:44.120-01:35:45.469 | Ayveen] In our mind.

[u1146 | 01:41:30.090-01:41:39.199 | Txarro] And the voice of Txarro begins speaking and tells the story of… A young man.

[u1149 | 01:41:44.150-01:41:48.630 | Txarro] Lizardfolk villages, the ancestor explains to this…
[u1150 | 01:41:49.610-01:41:53.910 | Txarro] entity that presumably doesn't understand them are…
[u1151 | 01:41:55.040-01:41:59.500 | Txarro] Places of abundance where life thrives.
[u1152 | 01:41:59.840-01:42:15.900 | Txarro] But places of order and rigidity, too, where everything must have its place. And the village is like the fish weir that we build on the river that brings food and nutrients and life, but traps everything that swims through it.

[u1156 | 01:42:38.720-01:42:40.289 | Txarro] And we could not let go.

[u1200 | 01:46:24.110-01:46:32.750 | DM] A patch of darkness in the shape of a child, standing on almost a hillside of roots and bone.

[u1211 | 01:47:17.490-01:47:28.049 | DM] Swift in a direction away from every other direction, going to a home that none of you know, but to which you each will go one day, and they're gone.

[u1223 | 01:48:22.800-01:48:26.899 | DM] And this, what you see, you DO remember.
[u1224 | 01:48:27.020-01:48:36.579 | DM] You remember, in every one of the worst moments of your life that come in all of the years that follow, this is what you see when you close your eyes. It is…
[u1225 | 01:48:36.800-01:48:47.820 | DM] An un-thing. Made of bone and bramble and tree roots and mold, shaped something like a dandelion made of darkness seven stories tall.
[u1226 | 01:48:47.900-01:49:03.540 | DM] Its roots, the size of elephants, are alive and writhing like slugs, like pale leeches exposed to the light, terrified, unthinking, reaching through the boundaries of the multiverse like living and clawed things.
[u1227 | 01:49:04.270-01:49:08.920 | DM] And the dandelion at the top, this pale sphere, spread out.
[u1228 | 01:49:09.300-01:49:26.040 | DM] At the end of each of those seeds are the faces of everyone you have ever met, trying to rip themselves free of the horror that they know that they are. A plant whose leaves around the base are tentacles of rot and bone and darkness, which, until recently.
[u1229 | 01:49:26.110-01:49:34.300 | DM] Wrapped themselves around the shimmering spheres of mortal souls which screamed for centuries without ever stopping.
[u1230 | 01:49:34.910-01:49:36.410 | DM] And what you know.
[u1231 | 01:49:36.600-01:49:49.449 | DM] In the darkness and the nightmare moments in the decades to come are that this thing that you perceive is only the closest approximation your mind can make of what it actually is.
[u1232 | 01:49:51.480-01:49:54.019 | DM] And then it's a child again.
[u1233 | 01:49:55.370-01:49:58.270 | DM] And the ruins and remains of the prison.

[u1244 | 01:50:54.180-01:50:58.529 | DM] On an empty hillside, In the gathering twilight.
[u1245 | 01:50:59.000-01:51:02.359 | DM] Just as the first stars of evening are beginning to come out.
[u1246 | 01:51:11.820-01:51:17.680 | DM] That's where we'll end, and I… I have a closing… montage, too.

[u1255 | 01:51:56.290-01:51:59.820 | DM] And out towards the horizon where the sun has set?
[u1256 | 01:52:01.620-01:52:03.689 | DM] And then pans back again.
[u1257 | 01:52:04.540-01:52:10.500 | DM] And it's the last of the sunset shining through a high window of an old house.
[u1258 | 01:52:10.670-01:52:14.810 | DM] Onto the bloody and exhausted form of Lord Hulda.
[u1259 | 01:52:15.210-01:52:31.049 | DM] Surrounded by the butchered corpse of the half-dozen mushroom men Aldens, who were his servants. And he drinks a potion, but his wounds don't fully close, and he coughs, and then he coughs again, and he doesn't stop coughing until he does, all at once.
[u1260 | 01:52:33.600-01:52:39.079 | DM] Below the old trees, on the wild edges of the Duchy of Tellim, it's always dusk.
[u1261 | 01:52:40.220-01:52:53.960 | DM] And in that twilight, the pale mushroom children of the thirst walk without sleeping or dreaming or hungering, and they flee from fire and iron, and they make new siblings out of anyone they find.
[u1262 | 01:52:54.980-01:53:02.700 | DM] And they search in those woods, always, obsessively, for the hidden door that will bring them back home to their parent again.
[u1263 | 01:53:06.110-01:53:12.289 | DM] In a room filled with glass tanks of fungus, Growing on severed limbs.
[u1264 | 01:53:13.960-01:53:18.949 | DM] Just as you feel the first rumbles start to vibrate.
[u1265 | 01:53:19.090-01:53:25.090 | DM] Through it, just as you can see the glass tanks shaking and the house is destroyed.
[u1266 | 01:53:26.020-01:53:40.799 | DM] A body in the shape of a dwarven woman, but made from fungi and lichens of every possible description, sits up and looks, for a long moment, at the lifeless corpse which once held the soul now encased in its own fungal golem flesh.
[u1267 | 01:53:41.940-01:53:57.510 | DM] And as the room around her shakes, as pieces of the ceiling begin to fall to the floor, Kallipseis, now wizard head of Arithrimos Lamperum in a golem body, consults a book filled with long lists of six-digit numbers and blinks out of sight.
[u1268 | 01:54:01.030-01:54:02.520 | DM] Camera pulls back.
[u1269 | 01:54:03.280-01:54:17.880 | DM] And it's a quiet, thatched cottage by the side of a road which runs parallel exactly to the sunset, and a tigerman is stowing in his bag the polished sphere of crystal through which he watched a golem plane shift out of the Feywild.
[u1270 | 01:54:18.560-01:54:21.390 | DM] And he draws thoughtfully from his pipe.
[u1271 | 01:54:22.140-01:54:23.600 | DM] and sighs.
[u1272 | 01:54:23.760-01:54:29.840 | DM] And at the corners of his mouth, a sly smile creeps across the massive tiger face.
[u1273 | 01:54:33.330-01:54:48.159 | DM] It is no time. It is not time, it is outside of time, in the black nothingness beyond the universe, where the black stars do not shine, and an un-thing that is sometimes a child, and sometimes a monster.
[u1274 | 01:54:48.160-01:54:59.999 | DM] can once again see the complete and unambiguous shape of all of its own possibilities. Its past, its present, its future, all at once, unhidden, always moving, never changing.
[u1275 | 01:55:01.520-01:55:04.859 | DM] And in that place, which is not a place.
[u1276 | 01:55:05.610-01:55:08.830 | DM] It doesn't think of the soul horde it kept.
[u1277 | 01:55:10.150-01:55:15.289 | DM] But of the teacher who found it as a lost child and loved it into speech.
[u1278 | 01:55:17.590-01:55:22.180 | DM] And finally… Finally.
[u1279 | 01:55:22.530-01:55:29.840 | DM] Under a twilight sky, Wide green lawns rise from the river to a beautiful manor house.
[u1280 | 01:55:30.520-01:55:32.380 | DM] Where a party has finished.
